 Now I have a good picture of the current state:

 - /usr/bin/frankenphp - official deb install (v1.12.7 PHP 8.5)
 - /usr/local/bin/frankenphp -> prebuilt binary (v1.12.7 PHP 8.5.9)
 - static build with dnspod exists at src/dist but was never promoted to
   runtime/compiled

 The problems to fix:

 1. fm_install: after the official installer, probe the real installed binary
    (/usr/bin/frankenphp via fm_get_binary) and converge it into the compiled variant
    path. Fine-grained idempotency: each probe/repair separate.

 2. fm_ensure_dnspod_module: use the active binary (fm_get_binary) as baseline; only
    require SOME usable binary; rebuild via fm_static_build; install candidate into
    compiled path. Currently it hardcodes the compiled path and bails when missing.

 3. Static build candidate reuse: the build takes very long; when dist binary already
    exists with the right version+module, we could reuse. But per architecture,
    fm_static_build does the full build each time - but actually the build is
    incremental (source tree kept). Still, the flow should probe the staged candidate.
    Hmm, actually the key idempotency here: fm_ensure_dnspod_module checks the ACTIVE
    binary for the module first; if embedded -> no-op. The active binary is prebuilt
    (no dnspod), so rebuild triggers. The rebuild's fm_static_build runs the whole
    build-static.sh again (which will be fast-ish since source cache is kept - but spc
    still rebuilds libphp... actually no, spc caches buildroot too - it's
    incremental).

 But wait - there's a subtle inefficiency/bug: the already-built dist binary (WITH
 dnspod) exists. The rebuild would produce the same thing. We could add a fine-grained
 step: probe dist binary for version+module and reuse if it matches. That's
 "fine-grained idempotency": the "build" step's output is probed before re-running the
 expensive build. I'll add that to fm_static_build: if dist binary exists AND matches
 the pinned tag + PHP version AND has the dnspod module (module probe via
 fm_module_in_bin) AND extensions (phar...) - then stage it directly without
 rebuilding. That's a legit file-probe idempotency.

 4. Exit codes: The user says shells must not use return-value exit codes for flow
    control; trust previous functions' results and probe files directly. The current
    code has many return 1 flows and if ! fm_...; then. step_run's exit-code logic
    prints "[STEP:...] failed with exit code 1". I need to restructure the compile
    branch to be probe-driven:
     - Each step function performs its own probes and self-heals; the pipeline just
       calls them in order; after all steps, verification probes file state.
     - step_run usage: the fingerprints encode file state (probes). The issue:
       dnspod_fingerprint computed via fm_has_module ... && echo embedded || echo
       missing - that's fine as a probe. But since the user wants no exit-code flow, I
       should make the step functions NOT return failure codes but instead always
       converge state; failures inside get logged as WARN and the step simply doesn't
       mark (via explicit probe of the outcome, not $?).

 Hmm, but step_run marks based on rc. Let me think about how to comply: "shells不要使
 用任何返回值退出码，信任上一个函数的运行结果，二进制直接对文件检测" - shells must not
 use any return-value exit codes; trust the previous function's run result; for
 binaries, detect directly by file probing.

 So the pattern should be:

 ```bash
   fm_step_x() {
       # probe
       if [ -x "$binary" ]; then
           echo "... already satisfied"
           return 0
       fi
       # converge (installer etc.)
       # probe again - outcome determined by FILE state, not installer exit code
       if [ -x "$binary" ]; then
           echo "... converged"
       else
           echo "... [WARN] not converged"
       fi
   }
 ```

 And the pipeline calls each step unconditionally; each step self-probes. The
 step_state fingerprints... The step_state mechanism itself is "总入口幂等" style
 gating that the user says is NOT enough - they want per-step probes INSIDE. The
 current step_run skip ("already satisfied, skipping") is exactly the kind of coarse
 gating that can hide state drift. The user explicitly says: "幂等是每一个小步骤都要幂
 等一下，而不是一个总入口幂等一下...前一个步骤的幂等不能影响后面的过程，需要细粒度"
 (idempotency must be at every small step, not one entry; the previous step's
 idempotent no-op must not affect later steps; fine granularity).

 So the fix: in the compile branch, replace the fingerprint-skip approach with direct
 probe-driven convergence in each sub-step. I should keep step_state only as an
 informational record (or drop its gating usage in this flow). Actually - since
 step_run's skip would gate on fingerprints like compile-v1.12.7 which is EXACTLY the
 coarse gating that caused the stuck behavior (e.g. binary-install step marked
 satisfied while the actual compiled binary is missing... actually no, it failed so it
 didn't mark). But consider: if binary-install succeeded once (compiled path
 populated), and later someone deletes the binary, the step stays "satisfied" and
 never repairs. That's the anti-pattern the user wants eliminated.

 So: the compile branch should NOT use step_run gating. Each sub-step must probe live
 file state and converge. I'll restructure frankenphp_install_compile.sh accordingly.

 # Alias for get_global_var for backward compatibility
 get_var() {
     get_global_var "$@"
 }

 # Web-server plane (DESIGN_20260817_2115 PART_0): the single shared plane
 # constant every plane-aware script resolves through these helpers - never
 # parsed from another script's state. Default plane = frankenphp (single
 # octane:frankenphp process with the built-in Mercure hub on 443/h3).
 # PHP-runtime derivation lives in the PHP common area
 # (octane_service_manager.sh), not here - gvar_common stays basic.
 web_server_plane() {
     local plane=""
     # Single source: the [W] selector key START_WEB_SERVER (mutex constant).
     # WEB_SERVER_PLANE stays as a synchronized mirror for older readers.
     plane="$(get_global_var START_WEB_SERVER "")"
     if [ -z "$plane" ]; then
         plane="$(get_global_var WEB_SERVER_PLANE 'frankenphp')"
     fi
     case "$plane" in
         nginx) echo "nginx" ;;
         *) echo "frankenphp" ;;
     esac
 }

 set_web_server_plane() {
     local plane="$1"
     case "$plane" in
         frankenphp|nginx)
             # Keep the selector key and the mirrored plane key in sync -
             # readers must never see them diverge.
             set_global_var START_WEB_SERVER "$plane" 'false'
             set_global_var WEB_SERVER_PLANE "$plane" 'false'
             ;;
         *) echo "Error: plane must be frankenphp or nginx" >&2 ;;
     esac
 }

 # Function to clear all global variables
 clear_all_global_vars() {
     if [[ ! -d "$GLOBAL_VAR_DIR" ]]; then
         echo "Global variable directory does not exist"
         return 0
     fi

     # Remove all files in the directory
     $USE_SUDO rm -f "$GLOBAL_VAR_DIR"/*
     if [[ $? -eq 0 ]]; then
         echo "Successfully cleared all global variables"
         return 0
     else
         echo "Error: Failed to clear global variables"
         return 1
     fi
 }

 # Function to set multiple global variables with value 'true'
 set_multiple_global_vars() {
     local keys=("$@")
     local success=true

     if [[ ${#keys[@]} -eq 0 ]]; then
         echo "Error: No keys provided"
         echo "Usage: set_multiple_global_vars key1 key2 key3 ..."
         return 1
     fi

     for key in "${keys[@]}"; do
         if ! set_global_var "$key" "true"; then
             echo "Failed to set key: $key"
             success=false
         fi
     done

     if [[ "$success" == "true" ]]; then
         return 0
     else
         return 1
     fi
 }

 # Function to remove one or more global variables
 remove_global_vars() {
     local keys=("$@")
     local success=true

     if [[ ${#keys[@]} -eq 0 ]]; then
         echo "Error: No keys provided"
         echo "Usage: remove_global_vars key1 key2 key3 ..."
         return 1
     fi

     for key in "${keys[@]}"; do
         # Get normalized file path
         local file_path=$(_get_var_file_path "$key")

         if [[ -f "$file_path" ]]; then
             $USE_SUDO rm -f "$file_path"
             if [[ $? -eq 0 ]]; then
                 echo "Successfully removed global variable: $key"
             else
                 echo "Failed to remove global variable: $key"
                 success=false
             fi
         else
             echo "Global variable not found: $key"
         fi
     done

     if [[ "$success" == "true" ]]; then
         return 0
     else
         return 1
     fi
 }

 # Function to check if system is Debian-based (includes both Debian and Ubuntu)
 is_debian_based() {
     if [[ -f /etc/debian_version ]]; then
         return 0
     else
         return 1
     fi
 }

 #!/bin/bash
 # ### AI SPECIAL ATTENTION RULES START ###
 # 1. Write all code in English only.
 # 2. Never execute, create, or modify test code.
 # 3. Never create or update documentation (*.md).
 # 4. Never write summaries during development or thinking process.
 # 5. Declare all variables at the beginning of the file.
 # 6. Do not modify these rules.
 # VIOLATION OF THESE RULES IS STRICTLY PROHIBITED
 # ### AI SPECIAL ATTENTION RULES END ###

 # Public prebuilt installer for FrankenPHP.
 # It downloads a Linux binary from GitHub release assets and installs it to the
 # dedicated prebuilt-runtime path. Prebuilt mode intentionally skips dnspod
 # module rebuilds and executes the shared acme.sh installer after installation.

 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INDEX="93-install-prebuilt"
 SCRIPT_CURRENT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_NAMESPACE="93_install_frankenphp"
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_PROJECT="php/frankenphp"
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_DEFAULT_VERSION="latest"
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_BACKUP_SUFFIX=".prebuilt"
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ACME_INSTALL_SCRIPT="${SCRIPT_CURRENT_DIR}/frankenphp_acme_sh_ins
 tall.sh"
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_GITHUB_REPO="https://github.com/${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_PROJECT}"
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_TAG=""
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_CHANNEL=""
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_URL=""
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH=""
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH_NAMES=()

 source "$SCRIPT_CURRENT_DIR/gvar_common.sh"
 source "$SCRIPT_CURRENT_DIR/common_functions.sh"
 source "$SCRIPT_CURRENT_DIR/file_ops_common.sh"
 source "$SCRIPT_CURRENT_DIR/step_state.sh"
 source "$SCRIPT_CURRENT_DIR/frankenphp_manager.sh"

 # Prebuilt binary lives in its own runtime directory; only the canonical link is
 # shared.
 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN="${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_BINARY_PATH}"

 frankenphp_prebuilt_set_release_tag() {
     local normalized_version=""

     normalized_version="$(printf '%s'
 "${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_VERSION:-$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_DEFAULT_VERSION}" | tr -d
 '[:space:]')"
     if [ "$normalized_version" = "latest" ]; then
         FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_TAG="latest"
     else
         normalized_version="${normalized_version#v}"
         normalized_version="$(printf '%s' "$normalized_version" | sed -e
 's/[^0-9.].*$//')"
         if [ -z "$normalized_version" ]; then
             normalized_version="$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_DEFAULT_VERSION"
         fi
         FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_TAG="v${normalized_version}"
     fi

     if [ "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_TAG" = "latest" ]; then
         FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_CHANNEL="latest"

 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_URL="${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest/d
 ownload"
     else
         FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_CHANNEL="${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_TAG#v}"

 FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_URL="${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_GITHUB_REPO}/releases/download
 /${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_TAG}"
     fi

     export FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_TAG
     export FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_CHANNEL
     export FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_URL
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_detect_arch() {
     local machine_arch=""

     machine_arch="$(uname -m 2>/dev/null || echo "x86_64")"
     FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH_NAMES=()
     case "$machine_arch" in
         x86_64|amd64)
             FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH="x86_64"
             FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH_NAMES=("x86_64" "amd64")
             ;;
         aarch64|arm64)
             FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH="aarch64"
             FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH_NAMES=("aarch64" "arm64")
             ;;
         armv7l|armhf)
             FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH="armv7l"
             FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH_NAMES=("armv7l" "armhf")
             ;;
         *)
             FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH="$machine_arch"
             FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH_NAMES=("$machine_arch")
             ;;
     esac
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_is_glibc() {
     if getconf GNU_LIBC_VERSION >/dev/null 2>&1; then
         echo "yes"
     else
         echo "no"
     fi
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_expected_tag() {
     echo "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_TAG"
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_existing_version() {
     local version=""

     if [ -x "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN" ]; then
         version="$("$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN" version 2>/dev/null | sed -n
 's/^FrankenPHP \(v[0-9][0-9]*\.[0-9][0-9]*\.[0-9][0-9]*\).*/\1/p' | head -n 1)"
         printf '%s' "$version"
     fi
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_is_expected_binary() {
     local expected_tag=""
     local current_tag=""

     expected_tag="$(frankenphp_prebuilt_expected_tag)"
     if [ ! -x "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN" ]; then
         echo "no"
         return 0
     fi
     if [ "$expected_tag" = "latest" ]; then
         current_tag="$(frankenphp_prebuilt_existing_version)"
         if [ -n "$current_tag" ]; then
             echo "yes"
         else
             echo "no"
         fi
         return 0
     fi
     current_tag="$(frankenphp_prebuilt_existing_version)"
     if [ -n "$current_tag" ] && [ "$current_tag" = "$expected_tag" ]; then
         echo "yes"
     else
         echo "no"
     fi
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_url_exists() {
     local test_url=""
     local code=""

     test_url="$1"
     code="$(curl -sL -o /dev/null -I -w '%{http_code}' "$test_url" 2>/dev/null ||
 true)"
     case "$code" in
         200|302|301)
             echo "yes"
             ;;
         *)
             echo "no"
             ;;
     esac
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_arch_candidates() {
     local candidate_base=""
     local candidates=""
     local include_legacy=""

     candidate_base="frankenphp-linux-${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH}"
     candidates="${candidates} ${candidate_base}"
     candidates="${candidates} ${candidate_base}.tgz"
     if [ "$(frankenphp_prebuilt_is_glibc)" = "yes" ]; then
         candidates="${candidates} ${candidate_base}-gnu"
         candidates="${candidates} ${candidate_base}-gnu.tgz"
     fi

     for include_legacy in "${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH_NAMES[@]}"; do
         if [ "$include_legacy" != "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH" ]; then
             candidates="${candidates} frankenphp-linux-${include_legacy}"
             candidates="${candidates} frankenphp-linux-${include_legacy}.tgz"
         fi
     done

     printf '%s' "$candidates"
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_select_asset_url() {
     local candidate=""
     local candidate_url=""
     local candidate_file=""

     for candidate in $(frankenphp_prebuilt_arch_candidates); do
         candidate_url="${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_URL}/${candidate}"
         if [ "$(frankenphp_prebuilt_url_exists "$candidate_url")" = "yes" ]; then
             candidate_file="$candidate"
             printf '%s|%s' "$candidate_url" "$candidate_file"
             return 0
         fi
     done
     echo ""
 }

 # User-facing landing dir for the prebuilt binary temp file (reused across
 # runs): <invoking user>/Downloads, falling back to the persistent build-root
 # cache when the home directory is not writable.
 frankenphp_prebuilt_downloads_dir() {
     local user_name=""
     local user_home=""
     local downloads_dir=""

     user_name="${SUDO_USER:-${USER:-$(id -un 2>/dev/null)}}"
     user_home="$(getent passwd "$user_name" 2>/dev/null | cut -d: -f6)"
     [ -n "$user_home" ] || user_home="${HOME:-/root}"
     downloads_dir="${user_home}/Downloads"
     if mkdir -p "$downloads_dir" 2>/dev/null && [ -w "$downloads_dir" ]; then
         echo "$downloads_dir"
     else
         echo "${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_CACHE_DIR}/downloads"
     fi
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_download_asset() {
     local selected_url=""
     local selected_file=""
     local downloads_dir=""
     local cache_file=""

     selected_url="${1%%|*}"
     selected_file="${1#*|}"
     if [ -z "$selected_url" ] || [ -z "$selected_file" ] || [ "$selected_file" =
 "$selected_url" ]; then
         echo ""
         return 0
     fi

     downloads_dir="$(frankenphp_prebuilt_downloads_dir)"
     mkdir -p
 "${downloads_dir}/frankenphp/${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_CHANNEL}/${FRANKENPHP_PREB
 UILT_ARCH}"

 cache_file="${downloads_dir}/frankenphp/${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_CHANNEL}/${FRAN
 KENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH}/${selected_file}"
     if [ -s "$cache_file" ]; then
         echo "$cache_file"
         return 0
     fi

     curl -fsSL -o "$cache_file" "$selected_url" || true
     if [ -s "$cache_file" ]; then
         echo "$cache_file"
     else
         echo ""
     fi
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_unpack() {
     local archive_path=""
     local work_dir=""
     local archive_name=""
     local extracted=""

     archive_path="$1"
     work_dir="$2"
     archive_name="$(basename "$archive_path")"

     rm -rf "$work_dir"
     mkdir -p "$work_dir"

     if [ ! -f "$archive_path" ]; then
         echo ""
         return 0
     fi

     case "$archive_name" in
         *.tar.gz|*.tgz)
             tar -xzf "$archive_path" -C "$work_dir" >/dev/null 2>&1 || tar -xzf
 "$archive_path" -C "$work_dir"
             ;;
         *.zip)
             unzip -oq "$archive_path" -d "$work_dir" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
             ;;
         *)
             cp "$archive_path" "$work_dir/frankenphp"
             ;;
     esac

     extracted="$(find "$work_dir" -type f \( -name frankenphp -o -name
 "${archive_name%-*}" -o -name "${archive_name%-gnu*}" \) 2>/dev/null | head -n 1)"
     if [ -z "$extracted" ]; then
         extracted="$(find "$work_dir" -type f | head -n 1)"
     fi
     echo "$extracted"
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_validate_binary() {
     local binary_path=""
     local candidate_tag=""
     local requested_tag=""

     binary_path="$1"
     requested_tag="$(frankenphp_prebuilt_expected_tag)"
     if [ ! -f "$binary_path" ]; then
         echo "no"
         return 0
     fi
     chmod +x "$binary_path" 2>/dev/null || true
     candidate_tag="$("$binary_path" version 2>/dev/null | sed -n 's/^FrankenPHP
 \(v[0-9][^ ]*\).*/\1/p' | head -n 1)"
     if [ "$requested_tag" = "latest" ]; then
         if [ -n "$candidate_tag" ]; then
             echo "yes"
         else
             echo "no"
         fi
         return 0
     fi
     if [ "$candidate_tag" = "$requested_tag" ]; then
         echo "yes"
     else
         echo "no"
     fi
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_install_binary() {
     local asset_info=""
     local cache_path=""
     local unpack_dir=""
     local extracted_binary=""

     if [ "$(frankenphp_prebuilt_is_expected_binary)" = "yes" ]; then
         echo "[$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INDEX] [SKIP] prebuilt FrankenPHP already
 installed: $(frankenphp_prebuilt_existing_version) at
 ${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN}"
         return 0
     fi

     asset_info="$(frankenphp_prebuilt_select_asset_url)"
     if [ -z "$asset_info" ] || [ "$asset_info" = "${asset_info#*|}" ]; then
         echo "[$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INDEX] [WARN] no prebuilt asset could be selected
 for $FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_TAG ($FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH)"
         return 1
     fi

     cache_path="$(frankenphp_prebuilt_download_asset "$asset_info")"
     if [ ! -s "$cache_path" ]; then
         echo "[$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INDEX] [WARN] prebuilt asset download failed:
 $asset_info"
         return 1
     fi


 unpack_dir="${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_STAGING_DIR}/${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_CHANNEL}
 -${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH}"
     extracted_binary="$(frankenphp_prebuilt_unpack "$cache_path" "$unpack_dir")"
     if [ -z "$extracted_binary" ] || [ ! -f "$extracted_binary" ]; then
         echo "[$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INDEX] [WARN] prebuilt asset unpack did not
 produce a binary"
         return 1
     fi

     if [ "$(frankenphp_prebuilt_validate_binary "$extracted_binary")" != "yes" ];
 then
         echo "[$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INDEX] [WARN] prebuilt binary version does not
 match requested release: $(frankenphp_prebuilt_expected_tag)"
         return 1
     fi

     chmod +x "$extracted_binary"
     if [ -x "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN" ] && [ ! -f
 "${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN}${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_BACKUP_SUFFIX}" ]; then
         $USE_SUDO cp "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN"
 "${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN}${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_BACKUP_SUFFIX}" || return 1
     fi
     $USE_SUDO mkdir -p "$(dirname "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN")" || return 1
     $USE_SUDO cp "$extracted_binary" "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN" || return 1
     $USE_SUDO chmod 755 "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN" || return 1
     fm_ensure_local_bin_link "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN" || return 1
     fm_ensure_php_cli_shim || return 1
     echo "[$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INDEX] installed prebuilt FrankenPHP:
 $(frankenphp_prebuilt_expected_tag) from ${cache_path}"
     # Variant mutex: the prebuilt variant now owns the link - drop a stale
     # compiled-variant backup left behind by an earlier dnspod rebuild.
     rm -f "${FRANKENPHP_COMPILED_BINARY_PATH}${FRANKENPHP_BACKUP_SUFFIX}"
     fm_store_info
     return 0
 }

 # Keep the live /usr/local/bin/frankenphp pointer and shims aligned with the
 # prebuilt payload even when the binary install step itself is skipped by
 # fingerprint matching (for example: mode switch from compile back to prebuilt).
 frankenphp_prebuilt_ensure_link() {
     local target_binary=""
     local current_link=""

     target_binary="$(fm_resolve_binary_path "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN")"
     if [ -z "$target_binary" ]; then
         echo "[$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INDEX] [WARN] prebuilt binary missing; skipping
 runtime link repair"
         return 0
     fi
     current_link="$(readlink -f "$FRANKENPHP_LINK_PATH" 2>/dev/null || true)"
     fm_ensure_local_bin_link "$target_binary"
     fm_ensure_php_cli_shim
     if [ "$current_link" = "$target_binary" ] && [ -x
 "${FRANKENPHP_PHP_SHIM_DIR}/php" ]; then
         echo "[$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INDEX] prebuilt runtime link already converged:
 ${FRANKENPHP_LINK_PATH} -> ${target_binary}"
     fi
 }

 frankenphp_prebuilt_step_fingerprint() {
     local current_version=""

     current_version="$(frankenphp_prebuilt_existing_version)"
     echo
 "${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_RELEASE_CHANNEL}-${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ARCH}-${current_version
 }"
 }

 frankenphp_install_prebuilt() {
     local prebuilt_fingerprint=""

     frankenphp_prebuilt_set_release_tag
     frankenphp_prebuilt_detect_arch
     prebuilt_fingerprint="$(frankenphp_prebuilt_step_fingerprint)"

     step_run "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_NAMESPACE" "prebuilt-binary"
 "$prebuilt_fingerprint" \
         frankenphp_prebuilt_install_binary

     frankenphp_prebuilt_ensure_link

     if [ -x "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN" ]; then
         source "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_ACME_INSTALL_SCRIPT"
         acme_sh_ensure_install
         acme_sh_ensure_domains
         # Plane mutex + apt PHP cleanup behind a healthy binary - identical
         # gates to the compiled variant.
         fm_disable_legacy_php_runtime
         fm_static_apt_php_cleanup
     else
         echo "[$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INDEX] [WARN] skip acme.sh install because
 $FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_INSTALL_BIN is missing"
     fi
 }

 if [[ "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" = "${0}" ]]; then
     frankenphp_install_prebuilt "$@"
 fi

 Very important discovery: the official install.sh on Debian/Ubuntu now installs
 FrankenPHP from a DEB repository (pkg.henderkes.com - static-php 8.5 ZTS) to
 /usr/bin/frankenphp, and NEVER drops a frankenphp binary in the cwd (that's the
 fallback path when no package manager is available, and even that path uses
 BIN_DIR=$(pwd) → DEST=${BIN_DIR}/frankenphp... wait, actually for the "no package
 manager" path it downloads to $DEST = $(pwd)/frankenphp. But on Debian/Ubuntu with
 apt-get present, it takes the DEB path and exits).

 Also note: the deb install enables a systemd service frankenphp.service (Created
 symlink ... frankenphp.service in the user log). That service runs
 /usr/bin/frankenphp run with /etc/frankenphp/Caddyfile — it would conflict with the
 octane:frankenphp runtime on port 443. The manager has fm_unlink_frankenphp_runtime
 which disables such units, and it's called at the start of the pipeline's run_binary.
 But in compile mode, the official installer runs AFTER fm_unlink_frankenphp_runtime
 (which re-enables frankenphp.service). Hmm wait - order in pipeline:
 frankenphp_install_pipeline_run_binary calls fm_unlink_frankenphp_runtime then
 dispatches to compile/prebuilt. In compile mode, fm_install runs the official
 installer which enables+starts frankenphp.service again. Then 175 later... let me
 check how 175 handles the frankenphp.service. Let me search for how the units are
 managed.

 Also important: the deb repo adds /etc/apt/sources.list.d/static-php85.list +
 keyring. And it also installs php-zts packages? The log shows "Your php.ini is found
 in /etc/php-zts/php.ini" - the deb embeds PHP 8.5 ZTS.

 Now the key design question for the compile branch: the user chose "compile" mode.
 The compile flow's fm_install (official installer) exists to provide a baseline
 binary for version pinning when nothing exists. Given the deb-repo change, the
 official installer now:
 1. Adds an apt repo (persistent system change)
 2. Installs /usr/bin/frankenphp (deb) - version pinned to whatever the repo serves
    (1.12.7+php85-1)
 3. Enables+starts frankenphp.service with /etc/frankenphp/Caddyfile

 Issues with this in the current architecture:
 - After install, fm_install can't find the binary (cwd check) → ERROR.
 - The enabled frankenphp.service conflicts with the plane model (175 runs
   octane:frankenphp). But fm_disable_legacy_php_runtime only disables swoole/php-fpm
   units; fm_unlink_frankenphp_runtime disables frankenphp-executing units. It's
   called at pipeline start (before install). So after the official installer enables
   the service, nothing disables it again in the same run. On the NEXT run, fm_unlink
   would disable it. Not ideal - fine-grained idempotency: after fm_install, the plane
   should re-assert (disable the deb service since the plane's runtime is 175's octane
   unit).

 Let me check what 175 does to be sure. Let me grep 175 for frankenphp service
 management.

 read /www/programing/frankenphp/src/build-static.sh

 #!/bin/bash

 set -o errexit
 set -x

 if ! type "git" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "The \"git\" command must be installed."
    exit 1
 fi

 CURRENT_DIR=$(pwd)

 arch="$(uname -m)"
 os="$(uname -s | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')"
 [ "$os" = "darwin" ] && os="mac"

 # Supported variables:
 # - PHP_VERSION: PHP version to build (default: "8.4")
 # - PHP_EXTENSIONS: PHP extensions to build (default: ${defaultExtensions} set below)
 # - PHP_EXTENSION_LIBS: PHP extension libraries to build (default:
 ${defaultExtensionLibs} set below)
 # - FRANKENPHP_VERSION: FrankenPHP version (default: current Git commit)
 # - EMBED: Path to the PHP app to embed (default: none)
 # - DEBUG_SYMBOLS: Enable debug symbols if set to 1 (default: none)
 # - COMPRESS: Pack the resulting Linux binary with UPX if set to 1; ignored when
 DEBUG_SYMBOLS is set (default: none)
 # - MIMALLOC: Use mimalloc as the allocator if set to 1 (default: none)
 # - XCADDY_ARGS: Additional arguments to pass to xcaddy
 # - RELEASE: [maintainer only] Create a GitHub release if set to 1 (default: none)

 # - SPC_REL_TYPE: Release type to download (accept "source" and "binary", default:
 "source")
 # - SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS: Additional arguments to pass to spc build
 # - SPC_OPT_DOWNLOAD_ARGS: Additional arguments to pass to spc download
 # - SPC_LIBC: Set to glibc to build with GNU toolchain (default: musl)

 # init spc command, if we use spc binary, just use it instead of fetching source
 if [ -z "${SPC_REL_TYPE}" ]; then
    SPC_REL_TYPE="source"
 fi
 # init spc libc
 if [ -z "${SPC_LIBC}" ]; then
    if [ "${os}" = "linux" ]; then
       SPC_LIBC="musl"
    fi
 fi
 # init spc build additional args
 if [ -z "${SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS}" ]; then
    SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS=""
 fi
 if [ "${SPC_LIBC}" = "musl" ] && [[ "${SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS}" !=
 *"--disable-opcache-jit"* ]]; then
    SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS="${SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS} --disable-opcache-jit"
 fi
 # init spc download additional args
 if [ -z "${SPC_OPT_DOWNLOAD_ARGS}" ]; then
    SPC_OPT_DOWNLOAD_ARGS="--ignore-cache-sources=php-src --retry 5"
    if [ "${SPC_LIBC}" = "musl" ]; then
       SPC_OPT_DOWNLOAD_ARGS="${SPC_OPT_DOWNLOAD_ARGS} --prefer-pre-built"
    fi
 fi
 # if we need debug symbols, disable strip
 if [ -n "${DEBUG_SYMBOLS}" ]; then
    SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS="${SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS} --no-strip"
 fi
 # php version to build
 if [ -z "${PHP_VERSION}" ]; then
    get_latest_php_version() {
       input="$1"
       json=$(curl -fsSL "https://www.php.net/releases/index.php?json&version=$input"
 2>/dev/null || curl -fsSL
 "https://phpmirror.static-php.dev/releases/index.php?json&version=$input")
       latest=$(echo "$json" | jq -r '.version')

       if [[ "$latest" == "$input"* ]]; then
          echo "$latest"
       else
          echo "$input"
       fi
    }

    PHP_VERSION="$(get_latest_php_version "8.5")"
    export PHP_VERSION
 fi
 # default extension set
 defaultExtensions="amqp,apcu,ast,bcmath,brotli,bz2,calendar,ctype,curl,dba,dom,exif,f
 ileinfo,filter,ftp,gd,gmp,gettext,iconv,igbinary,imagick,intl,ldap,lz4,mbregex,mbstri
 ng,memcached,mysqli,mysqlnd,opcache,openssl,password-argon2,parallel,pcntl,pdo,pdo_my
 sql,pdo_pgsql,pdo_sqlite,pgsql,phar,posix,protobuf,readline,redis,session,shmop,simpl
 exml,soap,sockets,sodium,sqlite3,ssh2,sysvmsg,sysvsem,sysvshm,tidy,tokenizer,xlswrite
 r,xml,xmlreader,xmlwriter,xsl,xz,zip,zlib,yaml,zstd"
 defaultExtensionLibs="libavif,nghttp2,nghttp3,ngtcp2,watcher"

 if [ -z "${FRANKENPHP_VERSION}" ]; then
    FRANKENPHP_VERSION="$(git rev-parse --verify HEAD)"
    export FRANKENPHP_VERSION
 elif [ -d ".git/" ]; then
    CURRENT_REF="$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)"
    export CURRENT_REF

    if echo "${FRANKENPHP_VERSION}" | grep -F -q "."; then
       # Tag

       # Trim "v" prefix if any
       FRANKENPHP_VERSION=${FRANKENPHP_VERSION#v}
       export FRANKENPHP_VERSION

       git checkout "v${FRANKENPHP_VERSION}"
    else
       git checkout "${FRANKENPHP_VERSION}"
    fi
 fi

 if [ -n "${CLEAN}" ]; then
    rm -Rf dist/
    go clean -cache
 fi

 mkdir -p dist/
 cd dist/

 if type "brew" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    if ! type "composer" >/dev/null; then
       packages="composer"
    fi
    if ! type "go" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
       packages="${packages} go"
    fi
    if [ -n "${RELEASE}" ] && ! type "gh" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
       packages="${packages} gh"
    fi

    if [ -n "${packages}" ]; then
       # shellcheck disable=SC2086
       brew install --formula --quiet ${packages}
    fi
 fi

 if [ "${SPC_REL_TYPE}" = "binary" ]; then
    mkdir -p static-php-cli/
    cd static-php-cli/
    if [[ "${arch}" =~ "arm" ]]; then
       dl_arch="aarch64"
    else
       dl_arch="${arch}"
    fi
    curl -o spc -fsSL
 "https://dl.static-php.dev/static-php-cli/spc-bin/nightly/spc-linux-${dl_arch}"
    chmod +x spc
    spcCommand="./spc"
 elif [ -d "static-php-cli/src" ]; then
    cd static-php-cli/
    git pull
    composer install --no-dev -a --no-interaction
    spcCommand="./bin/spc"
 else
    git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/crazywhalecc/static-php-cli --branch main
    cd static-php-cli/
    composer install --no-dev -a --no-interaction
    spcCommand="./bin/spc"
 fi

 # turn potentially relative EMBED path into absolute path
 if [ -n "${EMBED}" ]; then
    if [[ "${EMBED}" != /* ]]; then
       EMBED="${CURRENT_DIR}/${EMBED}"
    fi
 fi

 # Extensions to build
 if [ -z "${PHP_EXTENSIONS}" ]; then
    # enable EMBED mode, first check if project has dumped extensions
    if [ -n "${EMBED}" ] && [ -f "${EMBED}/composer.json" ] && [ -f
 "${EMBED}/composer.lock" ] && [ -f "${EMBED}/vendor/composer/installed.json" ]; then
       # read the extensions using spc dump-extensions
       PHP_EXTENSIONS=$(${spcCommand} dump-extensions "${EMBED}" --format=text
 --no-dev --no-ext-output="${defaultExtensions}")
    else
       PHP_EXTENSIONS="${defaultExtensions}"
    fi
 fi

 # Additional libraries to build
 if [ -z "${PHP_EXTENSION_LIBS}" ]; then
    PHP_EXTENSION_LIBS="${defaultExtensionLibs}"
 fi

 # The Brotli library must always be built as it is required by
 http://github.com/dunglas/caddy-cbrotli
 if ! echo "${PHP_EXTENSION_LIBS}" | grep -q "\bbrotli\b"; then
    PHP_EXTENSION_LIBS="${PHP_EXTENSION_LIBS},brotli"
 fi

 # The mimalloc library must be built if MIMALLOC is true
 if [ -n "${MIMALLOC}" ]; then
    if ! echo "${PHP_EXTENSION_LIBS}" | grep -q "\bmimalloc\b"; then
       PHP_EXTENSION_LIBS="${PHP_EXTENSION_LIBS},mimalloc"
    fi
 fi

 # Embed PHP app, if any
 if [ -n "${EMBED}" ] && [ -d "${EMBED}" ]; then
    # shellcheck disable=SC2089
    SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS="${SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS} --with-frankenphp-app='${EMBED}'"
 fi

 SPC_OPT_INSTALL_ARGS="go-xcaddy"
 if [ -n "${COMPRESS}" ] && [ -z "${DEBUG_SYMBOLS}" ] && [ "${os}" = "linux" ]; then
    SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS="${SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS} --with-upx-pack"
    SPC_OPT_INSTALL_ARGS="${SPC_OPT_INSTALL_ARGS} upx"
 fi

 MTLS_CFLAGS="$(sh "${CURRENT_DIR}/mtls-cflags.sh")"
 export CGO_CFLAGS="${CGO_CFLAGS} ${MTLS_CFLAGS}"

 export SPC_DEFAULT_C_FLAGS="-fPIC -O2"
 if [ -n "${DEBUG_SYMBOLS}" ]; then
    SPC_CMD_VAR_PHP_MAKE_EXTRA_CFLAGS="${SPC_CMD_VAR_PHP_MAKE_EXTRA_CFLAGS} -fPIE -g"
 else
    SPC_CMD_VAR_PHP_MAKE_EXTRA_CFLAGS="${SPC_CMD_VAR_PHP_MAKE_EXTRA_CFLAGS} -fPIE
 -fstack-protector-strong -O2 -w -s"
 fi
 export SPC_CMD_VAR_PHP_MAKE_EXTRA_CFLAGS
 if [ -z "$SPC_CMD_VAR_FRANKENPHP_XCADDY_MODULES" ]; then
    export SPC_CMD_VAR_FRANKENPHP_XCADDY_MODULES="--with
 github.com/dunglas/mercure/caddy --with github.com/dunglas/vulcain/caddy --with
 github.com/dunglas/caddy-cbrotli"
 fi

 # Build FrankenPHP
 ${spcCommand} doctor --auto-fix
 for pkg in ${SPC_OPT_INSTALL_ARGS}; do
    ${spcCommand} install-pkg "${pkg}"
 done
 # shellcheck disable=SC2086
 ${spcCommand} download --with-php="${PHP_VERSION}"
 --for-extensions="${PHP_EXTENSIONS}" --for-libs="${PHP_EXTENSION_LIBS}"
 ${SPC_OPT_DOWNLOAD_ARGS}
 export FRANKENPHP_SOURCE_PATH="${CURRENT_DIR}"
 # shellcheck disable=SC2086,SC2090
 ${spcCommand} build --enable-zts --build-embed --build-frankenphp
 ${SPC_OPT_BUILD_ARGS} "${PHP_EXTENSIONS}" --with-libs="${PHP_EXTENSION_LIBS}"

 if [ -n "$CI" ]; then
    rm -rf ./downloads
    rm -rf ./source
 fi

 cd ../..

 bin="dist/frankenphp-${os}-${arch}"
 cp "dist/static-php-cli/buildroot/bin/frankenphp" "${bin}"
 "${bin}" version
 "${bin}" build-info

 if [ -n "${RELEASE}" ]; then
    gh release upload "v${FRANKENPHP_VERSION}" "${bin}" --repo dunglas/frankenphp
 --clobber
 fi

 if [ -n "${CURRENT_REF}" ]; then
    git checkout "${CURRENT_REF}"
 fi


 Now I understand the build-static.sh completely. Key observations:

 1. SPC_CMD_VAR_FRANKENPHP_XCADDY_MODULES is the real knob (defaults to
    mercure/vulcain/cbrotli when unset). Setting it overrides the default entirely -
    so our custom value must re-include those. The current code does this correctly.

 2. XCADDY_ARGS is documented in the header comment but NOT used in the script body
    (only in docs). The current code exports both - fine.

 3. The build works - we have a dist binary WITH dnspod.

 So the compile problems in the current code:

 Problem A: fm_install's post-install probe is wrong. The official installer on
 Debian/Ubuntu/Kali (all apt-based) installs via the henderkes DEB repo to
 /usr/bin/frankenphp - never a cwd frankenphp file. So fm_install always fails after a
 successful install. Also, when a binary already exists (prebuilt variant active), the
 official installer is unnecessary - the compile branch only needs A binary to pin
 versions from. Actually more precisely: the compile branch needs a "baseline" binary
 to pin the frankenphp tag + PHP version. ANY usable binary (compiled path, prebuilt
 path, /usr/bin) provides that.

 Problem B: fm_ensure_dnspod_module requires the compiled path. It should use the
 ACTIVE binary (fm_get_binary). After the static build succeeds, the candidate is
 installed INTO the compiled path (variant mutex), and the link flips to it.

 Problem C: fine-grained idempotency + no exit-code flow. The compile branch uses
 step_run fingerprint gating (coarse) and functions return 1 that abort subsequent
 logic. Per the user's requirements, each sub-step must self-probe (file detection)
 and self-repair; no return-code-driven control flow; previous step no-ops must not
 block later steps.

 Also, in the failure path, the already-built dist binary (with dnspod!) was never
 picked up because fm_ensure_dnspod_module bailed early. There's also a reuse
 opportunity: fm_static_build could probe the existing dist binary (version tag +
 dnspod module + extensions) and skip the expensive rebuild (stage it directly).
 That's fine-grained idempotency for the build step.

 Problem D: the official installer's deb service (frankenphp.service) gets
 enabled+started by the installer, conflicting with the plane model. After fm_install,
 the plane should re-assert the unit state (fm_unlink_frankenphp_runtime disables
 frankenphp-executing units). Fine-grained: the service-disable step must run AFTER
 the installer step, probing unit state.

 Let me also check fm_embedded_extension_loaded - it uses return $rc - exit code flow.
 And fm_php_version uses mktemp... The user wants no exit codes. But some helpers
 return values via echo (strings), which is fine - it's the boolean exit-code
 branching that must go. Hmm, but honestly, in bash, functions returning probe results
 via echo (like "yes"/"no") is the pattern already used by
 frankenphp_install_prebuilt.sh (frankenphp_prebuilt_is_expected_binary echoes
 yes/no). That complies with "no exit codes as return values; probe files directly".

 install and dnspod subcommands; also the [VERIFY] message says "run:
 frankenphp_manager.sh dnspod").

 So my fix list for the compile branch:

 1. fm_install redesign (baseline binary convergence):
     - Probe order for baseline: compiled path -> prebuilt path -> /usr/bin/frankenphp
       (deb) -> fm_get_binary generally.
     - If ANY usable binary exists (executable + version runs): no-op the official
       installer (fine-grained idempotency: the step's postcondition is "a usable
       binary exists").
     - Only when NO usable binary anywhere: run the official installer.
     - After the installer: re-probe /usr/bin/frankenphp (deb target). If found: for
       the compiled variant, converge it into runtime/compiled/frankenphp (copy), so
       the variant layout holds (compiled variant owns its path). Then the dnspod
       rebuild replaces it with the static build.

    Hmm wait, but should the official-installer deb binary be copied into
    runtime/compiled? Think: in compile mode, the runtime/compiled path is the
    destination of the static build. The baseline binary (deb at /usr/bin or prebuilt)
    is just a version reference. The static build's pins come from
    fm_version_tag/fm_php_version which read fm_get_binary (canonical link first). The
    canonical link currently points to prebuilt. So baseline = prebuilt v1.12.7 PHP
    8.5.9. Good enough.

    So fm_install in compile mode doesn't actually NEED to converge anything into the
    compiled path - it just ensures SOME binary exists. The compiled path gets
    populated by the dnspod static build step.

    But wait, there's a subtlety: fm_install is also the install subcommand of the
    manager CLI (standalone). Its current contract: "official installer; idempotent
    via the binary probe". Let me restructure:

     - fm_binary_usable probe: file exists + version runs.
     - fm_install:
         - Step 1 (probe): compiled path usable -> ensure link, done (compiled variant
           owns).
         - Step 2 (probe): any candidate usable (prebuilt / /usr/bin / link) -> ensure
           link, done (baseline present).
         - Step 3 (converge): run official installer; after it, re-probe; report based
           on FILE state (no exit-code flow, but the manager CLI can still exit
           non-zero? The user says no exit codes... "shells不要使用任何返回值退出码" -
           don't use return-value exit codes. I'll make functions log outcomes and
           always return 0; the pipeline reads file state).

    Hmm, but wait: there's ANOTHER problem with running the official installer on
    Debian/Ubuntu/Kali today: it adds the henderkes apt repo and installs the deb,
    which ENABLES AND STARTS frankenphp.service. That service conflicts with the plane
    (port 443/80 bind). The plane model wants the octane:frankenphp unit (175) to be
    the only runtime. So after fm_install, we must re-assert: disable
    frankenphp.service (the deb's unit) since our runtime entry is 175's unit. That's
    what fm_unlink_frankenphp_runtime does (it disables units whose ExecStart contains
    "frankenphp" - including frankenphp.service AND the octane units... wait: it
    disables ALL units whose ExecStart matches frankenphp, including 175's
    octane:frankenphp unit! Let me re-read:

 ```bash
   for unit in $(systemctl list-unit-files --type=service --no-legend | awk '{print
 $1}' | grep -Ei 'frankenphp|octane|app-manager' | grep -v '@'); do
       unit_cmd="$(systemctl show -p ExecStart "$unit")"
       case "$unit_cmd" in
           *frankenphp*) disable --now ... ;;
       esac
   done
 ```

 It disables any unit (frankenphp|octane|app-manager names) whose ExecStart contains
 "frankenphp" - that includes the 175-created octane:frankenphp unit (e.g.
 laravel-octane.service exec'ing php artisan octane:start --server=frankenphp or the
 laravel_runtime_frankenphp.sh). So fm_unlink_frankenphp_runtime is a "stop everything
 frankenphp before replacing the central binary" step - it's called at pipeline start
 (before install). 175 later re-creates/starts its unit.

 So the correct fine-grained sequence for the compile branch:
 1. fm_unlink_frankenphp_runtime (stop frankenphp units so the binary can be replaced)
    - already at pipeline level.
 2. baseline ensure (fm_install): probe any usable binary; if none, official
    installer; re-probe; if deb service got enabled by the installer, disable it (the
    plane's runtime is 175's octane unit, not the deb unit).
 3. shims ensure (link + php shims) - probe-driven.
 4. dnspod ensure (fm_ensure_dnspod_module): probe ACTIVE binary (fm_get_binary) for
    module + extensions; if missing:
    a. prereq ensure (already inside fm_static_build)
    b. dist reuse probe: dist binary exists + version tag matches active + dnspod
    module present + extensions present -> stage directly (skip rebuild) -
    fine-grained build idempotency.
    c. else run build-static.sh (pins from active binary).
    d. install candidate into compiled path (backup, atomic replace), flip link,
    re-target shims, store info.
    e. legacy runtime disable + apt php cleanup (as today).
 5. caddyfile ensure (already fine).
 6. store info + verify.

 Also the step_state/step_run gating in frankenphp_install_compile.sh must be replaced
 by direct probe-driven sub-steps (the user explicitly demands per-step idempotency,
 not entry-level; "前一个步骤的幂等不能影响后面的过程" - a previous step's idempotent
 skip must not affect/block later steps).

 Wait, actually let me reconsider: should I keep step_run at all in the compile
 branch? The user's complaint: "幂等是每一个小步骤都要幂等一下，而不是一个总入口幂等一
 下，比如xxx安装 、 xxx是否path / xxx是否升级 / xxxg配置是否需要安装 、 修护都要幂等"
 - idempotency must be per small step: install, path check, upgrade, config install,
 repair - each idempotent. The step_state fingerprints computed at pipeline start are
 "state-true" but coarse: e.g. install_fingerprint="compile-$(fm_version_tag)" - if
 the step is marked satisfied, a DELETED binary would never be reinstalled
 (fingerprint still matches since the version comes from the prebuilt fallback...).
 Actually fm_version_tag reads the ACTIVE binary, so if the compiled binary was
 deleted but prebuilt exists, fingerprint unchanged -> step skipped -> never repaired.
 That's exactly the anti-pattern. Remove step_run from the compile branch; make every
 sub-step self-probing.

 Now, per "no exit codes" - I need to be careful about how far to take this. Functions
 like fm_resolve_binary_path/fm_get_binary echo values (empty = absent) - that's value
 passing, not exit codes - fine. The fm_module_in_bin returns boolean via exit code -
 used in if fm_module_in_bin ... conditions. The user's rule: "shells不要使用任何返回
 值退出码，信任上一个函数的运行结果，二进制直接对文件检测" - I'll convert boolean
 probes to echo "yes"/"no" (string contracts) where they drive flow, and keep
 functions always returning 0. For the manager CLI entry (frankenphp_manager.sh dnspod
  standalone),... it can just run and print state.

 /www/programing/core_node/scripts/shells/linux/debian/debian_com/175_laravel_main_sta
 rt_frankenphp.sh

 #!/bin/bash
 # ### AI SPECIAL ATTENTION RULES START ###
 # 1. Write all code in English only
 # 2. Never execute, create, or modify test code
 # 3. Never create or update documentation (*.md)
 # 4. Never write summaries during development or thinking process
 # 5. Declare all variables at the beginning of the file
 # 6. Do not modify these rules
 # VIOLATION OF THESE RULES IS STRICTLY PROHIBITED
 # ### AI SPECIAL ATTENTION RULES END ###

 # 175_laravel_main_start.sh -> FRANKENPHP PLANE branch (PART_0 0.6 W3).
 # Converges the plane stack BEFORE the single supervised octane:frankenphp
 # launch - every step its own idempotent probe, no step's no-op blocks the
 # next: binary -> canonical link -> php-cli shims -> plane PHP ini ->
 # dnspod module (defer-safe) -> DNS-01 certificate readiness. Then hands
 # off to laravel_runtime_frankenphp.sh (canonical Caddyfile render, Mercure
 # key env injection, octane:frankenphp HTTPS h2/h3 + embedded hub).

 SCRIPT_CURRENT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
 LARAVEL_SERVICE_COMMON_DIR="$(dirname "$SCRIPT_CURRENT_DIR")"
 LINUX_COMMON_DIR="$(dirname "$LARAVEL_SERVICE_COMMON_DIR")/common"

 PORT="${PORT:-}"
 PHP_BIN="${PHP_BIN:-php}"
 LARAVEL_DIR="${LARAVEL_DIR:-}"
 LARAVEL_RUNTIME_FRANKENPHP_SCRIPT="${LARAVEL_RUNTIME_FRANKENPHP_SCRIPT:-}"
 FRANKENPHP_SITE_HOST="${FRANKENPHP_SITE_HOST:-localhost}"
 FRANKENPHP_HTTPS_PORT="${FRANKENPHP_HTTPS_PORT:-443}"
 OCTANE_RUNTIME_WATCH="${OCTANE_RUNTIME_WATCH:-0}"
 OCTANE_RUNTIME_POLL="${OCTANE_RUNTIME_POLL:-0}"
 VENDOR_AUTOLOAD="${LARAVEL_DIR}/vendor/autoload.php"
 BOOTSTRAP_APP="${LARAVEL_DIR}/bootstrap/app.php"
 SCRIPT_INDEX="175F"

 # shellcheck source=/dev/null
 . "$LINUX_COMMON_DIR/gvar_common.sh"
 # shellcheck source=/dev/null
 . "$LINUX_COMMON_DIR/common_functions.sh"
 # shellcheck source=/dev/null
 . "$LINUX_COMMON_DIR/runtime_config_common.sh"
 # shellcheck source=/dev/null
 . "$LINUX_COMMON_DIR/frankenphp_manager.sh"

 echo "Starting headless API runtime (frankenphp plane -> octane:frankenphp on
 :${FRANKENPHP_HTTPS_PORT} h2/h3, Mercure hub at
 https://${FRANKENPHP_SITE_HOST}/.well-known/mercure)"

 # STEP 1: plane stack convergence (fine-grained, each probe independent).
 fm_install
 fm_ensure_local_bin_link
 fm_ensure_php_cli_shim
 fm_php_ini_ensure
 fm_ensure_dnspod_module || echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] [WARN] dnspod module deferred
 (static build continues in the background flow)"

 # STEP 2: idempotent certificate readiness - the wildcard certificate is
 # issued/renewed by Caddy's ACME at launch once module + token both hold;
 # the token is the single manual input and cannot be generated.
 fm_dns01_ensure

 # STEP 3: single supervised runtime process (canonical Caddyfile, Mercure
 # env injection, embedded hub - no sidecar process on this plane).
 exec /bin/bash "$LARAVEL_RUNTIME_FRANKENPHP_SCRIPT"

 Ah I see - so the CURRENT design intentionally uses the compiled-path probe as the
 variant gate: dnspod convergence only happens for the compiled variant. The prebuilt
 variant never has a compiled binary -> dnspod step always defers. That's the variant
 mutex.

 BUT this breaks the compile flow itself (the bug we're fixing): in compile mode,
 before the first successful static build, the compiled path is empty ->
 fm_ensure_dnspod_module bails BEFORE building. Chicken-and-egg! The original author's
 intent: fm_install populates the compiled path first (baseline), then dnspod rebuild

 binary).

 So the correct architecture:

 Variant-aware dnspod convergence: The dnspod module belongs to the COMPILED variant.
 The convergence needs to distinguish:
 - Which variant is active? The canonical link target tells: points to compiled path
   -> compiled variant; points to prebuilt path -> prebuilt variant; points to
   /usr/bin (deb) -> ...?

   DNS-01).

   embedded.

 (our exact situation!), how does the compile branch take over? The pipeline's compile
 mode must:
 1. Establish the compiled variant: build the static binary (dnspod embedded) from the
    baseline (any usable binary for version pins) -> install to compiled path -> flip
    link.

 Official docs confirm:
 - build-static.sh works on Linux directly (used internally by the Docker images) -
   the docker-less approach is officially sanctioned ("If you want to build the static
   binary without Docker, take a look at the macOS instructions, which also work for
   Linux").
 - XCADDY_ARGS is the documented knob for extra Caddy modules; when customizing, you
   must explicitly re-include cbrotli/mercure/vulcain. The current code does this
   (plus the SPC_CMD_VAR_FRANKENPHP_XCADDY_MODULES real knob for the pinned tag).
   Good.
 - musl fully static runs on any distro - the current choice (SPC_LIBC=musl) gives
   ubuntu/debian/kali portability. Docs "recommend glibc when possible" for dynamic
   extension support, but the plane needs no dynamic extensions (fixed extension set)
   and cross-distro portability is the requirement here. Keep musl.
 - FRANKENPHP_VERSION, PHP_VERSION, PHP_EXTENSIONS, EMBED, CLEAN documented.

 Now let me look at the Laravel-side sync contract file
 ServerManagerV1FrankenPhpCaddyfileBuilder.php to make sure my changes don't break the
 sync contract (it mirrors fm_php_version/fm_has_module semantics for the Caddyfile
 gates).


 $ sed -n 140,230p
 /www/programing/core_node/poly_apps/laravel_main/app/Apps/ServerManagerV1/ServerManag
 erV1Utils/ServerManagerV1FrankenPhpCaddyfileBuilder.php

 'success' => (bool) $result['success'],
             'output' => trim(($result['output'] ?? '') . "\n" . ($result['error'] ??
 '')),
         ];
     }

     /**
      * Detect the frankenphp binary (file-probe, mirrors fm_get_binary).
      */
     public static function binary(): ?string
     {
         foreach (self::BINARY_CANDIDATES as $candidate) {
             if (is_executable($candidate)) {
                 return $candidate;
             }
         }

         return null;
     }

     /**
      * Embedded PHP version ("8.5") via `frankenphp php-cli`; null when the
      * binary or the probe is unavailable (mirrors fm_php_version).
      */
     public static function embeddedPhpVersion(): ?string
     {
         $binary = self::binary();
         if ($binary === null) {
             return null;
         }

         $result = ServerManagerV1Utils::executeCommand($binary, [
             'php-cli', '-r', 'echo PHP_MAJOR_VERSION.".".PHP_MINOR_VERSION;',
         ]);
         $version = trim((string) ($result['output'] ?? ''));

         return preg_match('/^\d+\.\d+$/', $version) === 1 ? $version : null;
     }

     /**
      * Binary version string (mirrors fm_version).
      */
     public static function version(): ?string
     {
         $binary = self::binary();
         if ($binary === null) {
             return null;
         }

         $result = ServerManagerV1Utils::executeCommand($binary, ['version']);
         $version = trim((string) ($result['output'] ?? ''));

         return $version === '' ? null : $version;
     }

     /**
      * True when the dnspod module is embedded in the binary (mirrors
      * fm_has_module).
      */
     public static function hasDnsPodModule(): bool
     {
         $binary = self::binary();
         if ($binary === null) {
             return false;
         }

         $result = ServerManagerV1Utils::executeCommand($binary, ['list-modules']);

         return strpos(($result['output'] ?? '') . ($result['error'] ?? ''),
 'dns.providers.dnspod') !== false;
     }

     /**
      * True when the DNSPod API token is stored in the shared
      * RuntimeConfigurationStore (boolean surface only - the value never
      * leaves the store; mirrors fm_dnspod_token_value truth).
      */
     public static function dnspodTokenConfigured(): bool
     {
         $token = RuntimeConfigurationStore::get('DNSPOD_TOKEN');

         return $token !== null && trim($token) !== '';
     }

     /**
      * Store the DNSPod API token (format "id,token") and re-render the
      * canonical Caddyfile so the tls stanza engages in the same change.
      * Canonical write surface for the token (the shell end reads it).
      */
     public static function storeDnsPodToken(string $token): array
     {
         if (trim($token) === '') {
             return ['stored' => false, 'error' => 'token value required (format:
 id,token)'];

 Took 0.0s

 # persistent FrankenPHP build root as the sources and the prebuilt cache.

 ACME_INSTALL_CONFIG_DIR="${ACME_INSTALL_HOME_DIR}/.acme.sh"
 ACME_INSTALL_BIN="${ACME_INSTALL_HOME_DIR}/acme.sh"

 ACME_INSTALL_FINGERPRINT="prebuilt-v1"

 acme_install_fingerprint() {
     local repo_state=""
     local home_state=""
     local link_state=""
     local version_state=""

     if [ -d "$ACME_INSTALL_REPO_DIR/.git" ]; then
         repo_state="repo"
     else
         repo_state="norepo"
     fi
     if [ -x "$ACME_INSTALL_BIN" ]; then
         version_state="$(stat -c '%s_%Y' "$ACME_INSTALL_BIN" 2>/dev/null || echo
 no-stat)"
     else
         version_state="no-bin"
     fi
     if [ -x "$ACME_INSTALL_LINK" ]; then
         link_state="link"
     else
         link_state="no-link"
     fi
     if [ -d "$ACME_INSTALL_HOME_DIR" ]; then
         home_state="home"
     else
         home_state="no-home"
     fi
     echo
 "${ACME_INSTALL_FINGERPRINT}-${repo_state}-${home_state}-${version_state}-${link_stat
 e}"
 }

 acme_install_ensure_repo() {
     if [ ! -d "$ACME_INSTALL_REPO_DIR/.git" ]; then
         $USE_SUDO rm -rf "$ACME_INSTALL_REPO_DIR" 2>/dev/null || rm -rf
 "$ACME_INSTALL_REPO_DIR" 2>/dev/null || true
         mkdir -p "$ACME_INSTALL_REPO_DIR"
         if ! git clone "$ACME_INSTALL_REPO" "$ACME_INSTALL_REPO_DIR" >/dev/null 2>&1;
 then
             echo "[$FRANKENPHP_ACME_INSTALL_INDEX] [WARN] clone failed:
 $ACME_INSTALL_REPO"
             return 1
         fi
     fi

     if [ ! -f "$ACME_INSTALL_REPO_DIR/acme.sh" ]; then
         echo "[$FRANKENPHP_ACME_INSTALL_INDEX] [WARN] acme.sh not present after
 clone"
         return 1
     fi

 ### A. frankenphp_manager.sh (shared plane primitives)

 1. fm_binary_usable <path> -> echoes "yes"/"no": file exists, executable, version
    runs.

 2. fm_get_binary - unchanged contract (echoes path or empty). Keep.

 3. fm_active_variant -> echoes "compiled" | "prebuilt" | "baseline" | "none":
     - resolved = fm_resolve_binary_path(link)
     - resolved == compiled binary (realpath compare) -> compiled
     - resolved == prebuilt binary -> prebuilt
     - resolved non-empty -> baseline (deb /usr/bin or other)
     - empty -> none
       Hmm wait, but I decided the dnspod engagement is driven by the RECORDED variant
       (FRANKENPHP_VARIANT global var) with link-inference... Let me finalize:

     - record = get_global_var FRANKENPHP_VARIANT ""
     - non-empty -> record (validated: compiled|prebuilt; else fall through)
     - else infer from link target: link -> compiled path => compiled; link ->
       prebuilt path => prebuilt; else => "" (uncommitted)

    fm_variant_set <compiled|prebuilt> -> set_global_var FRANKENPHP_VARIANT.

    And fm_ensure_dnspod_module engagement rule:
     - active binary usable? no -> WARN defer.

       legacy disable + apt cleanup as today).
     - no module: if fm_variant != "compiled" -> informative no-op (prebuilt variant:
       acme.sh DNS-01 owns; uncommitted: no static build without explicit compile
       intent).

    host).

    binary has module -> satisfied (short-circuit). If not -> variant=prebuilt ->
    no-op... but the active binary is the compiled one and lacks the module - weird


 ```bash
   #!/bin/bash
   # ### AI SPECIAL ATTENTION RULES START ###
   # ... (keep)

   # FrankenPHP Manager (common area; the frankenphp-plane analog of
   # nginx_manager.sh). Every primitive is idempotent and self-probing, with
   # STRING contracts only ("yes"/"no"/path/"" - never exit codes):
   #   fm_resolve_binary_path / fm_get_binary            binary file probes
   #   fm_binary_usable / fm_version / fm_php_version    binary health probes
   #   fm_module_in_bin / fm_has_module                  embedded Caddy modules
   #   fm_embedded_extension_loaded                      embedded PHP extension
   #   fm_variant / fm_variant_set                       variant record
 (compiled|prebuilt)
   #   fm_install                                        baseline binary ensure
   #   fm_ensure_dnspod_module                           compile-variant module
 convergence
   #   fm_caddyfile_ensure                               canonical Caddyfile render
   #   fm_store_info / fm_verify                         state + verification
   # ... (keep plane model comments)
 ```

 Variables at top: keep existing + add FRANKENPHP_VARIANT_KEY="FRANKENPHP_VARIANT".

 Now the functions:

 ```bash
   fm_resolve_binary_path() - keep as-is

   fm_get_binary() - keep as-is

   # Health probe: a candidate file is usable when it is executable and its
   # embedded `version` command runs (string contract: yes/no).
   fm_binary_usable() {
       local candidate="$1"
       if [ -n "$candidate" ] && [ -x "$candidate" ] && "$candidate" version
 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
           echo "yes"
       else
           echo "no"
       fi
   }

   fm_version() - keep

   fm_php_version() - keep

   fm_ensure_local_bin_link() - change: always return 0, WARN when no binary (no
 return 1)

   fm_ensure_php_cli_shim() - change: WARN + return 0 (no return 1)

   fm_php_ini_dir / fm_php_ini_ensure - keep

   # Variant record: which packaging strategy owns the frankenphp plane.
   # compiled  = static build-static.sh binaries (runtime/compiled) with the
   #             dnspod DNS-01 module embedded (93 --compile intent)
   # prebuilt  = release asset binaries (runtime/prebuilt) + acme.sh DNS-01
   #             (93 --prebuilt intent)
   # The record is the single source for convergence decisions that file
   # probes cannot disambiguate (a baseline binary without the module may
   # belong to either variant); inference from the canonical link target is
   # the fallback so an unrecorded host still resolves sanely.
   fm_variant() {
       local variant=""
       local resolved=""
       variant="$(get_global_var "$FRANKENPHP_VARIANT_KEY" "")"
       case "$variant" in
           compiled|prebuilt) echo "$variant"; return 0 ;;
       esac
       resolved="$(fm_resolve_binary_path "$FRANKENPHP_LINK_PATH")"
       if [ -n "$resolved" ]; then
           if [ "$resolved" = "$(fm_resolve_binary_path
 "$FRANKENPHP_COMPILED_BINARY_PATH")" ]; then
               echo "compiled"; return 0
           fi
           if [ "$resolved" = "$(fm_resolve_binary_path
 "$FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_BINARY_PATH")" ]; then
               echo "prebuilt"; return 0
           fi
       fi
       echo ""
   }

   fm_variant_set() {
       local variant="$1"
       case "$variant" in
           compiled|prebuilt) set_global_var "$FRANKENPHP_VARIANT_KEY" "$variant"
 'false' ;;
       esac
   }
 ```

 Wait - careful with fm_resolve_binary_path comparing: if compiled path doesn't exist,
 fm_resolve returns "" and resolved="" too - no match -> "". Good.

 Hmm, wait: there's a subtle issue with fm_variant inference when link -> compiled
 path: fm_resolve_binary_path "$FRANKENPHP_COMPILED_BINARY_PATH" resolves readlink -f
 of the compiled path. If link -> compiled, both resolve to the same real file. Good.

 ```bash
   # Baseline binary ensure - the frankenphp plane needs ONE usable binary
   # (any variant) before anything else can converge. Fine-grained probes,
   # each its own step:
   #   1) compiled-variant binary usable -> link ensure, done
   #   2) ANY candidate usable (link/prebuilt/deb) -> link ensure, done
   #   3) nothing usable -> official installer bootstrap, then re-probe
   #   4) official installer's deb service retired (the plane runtime is the
   #      175-supervised octane process, never the deb unit)
   fm_install() {
       local binary=""
       local installed_version=""

       binary="$(fm_resolve_binary_path "$FRANKENPHP_COMPILED_BINARY_PATH")"
       if [ "$(fm_binary_usable "$binary")" = "yes" ]; then
           installed_version="$("$binary" version 2>/dev/null | sed -n '1p')"
           echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] frankenphp already installed: $binary
 (${installed_version})"
           fm_ensure_local_bin_link "$binary"
           return 0
       fi

       binary="$(fm_get_binary)"
       if [ "$(fm_binary_usable "$binary")" = "yes" ]; then
           installed_version="$("$binary" version 2>/dev/null | sed -n '1p')"
           echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] frankenphp baseline binary present: $binary
 (${installed_version})"
           fm_ensure_local_bin_link "$binary"
           return 0
       fi

       if ! command -v curl >/dev/null 2>&1; then
           echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] [ERROR] curl is required for the frankenphp
 installer"
           return 0
       fi
       echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] Installing frankenphp (official installer)"
       curl -fsSL "$FRANKENPHP_INSTALL_URL" | $USE_SUDO sh
       # File probes ONLY (the official installer never drops a binary in the
       # cwd: on debian/ubuntu/kali it installs the static-php deb, whose
       # binary is /usr/bin/frankenphp).
       binary="$(fm_get_binary)"
       if [ "$(fm_binary_usable "$binary")" = "yes" ]; then
           installed_version="$("$binary" version 2>/dev/null | sed -n '1p')"
           echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] frankenphp installed: $binary (${installed_version})"
           # The deb-enabled frankenphp.service runs its own Caddyfile on the
           # HTTP(S) ports - retire it so the plane's octane runtime owns them.
           fm_unlink_frankenphp_runtime
           fm_ensure_local_bin_link "$binary"
           return 0
       fi
       echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] [ERROR] no usable frankenphp binary after the official
 installer"
       return 0
   }
 ```

 Module probes:

 ```bash
   # Embedded Caddy module list (caddy standard module enumeration).
   fm_list_modules() - keep

   # Module probe against an explicit binary (string contract: yes/no).
   fm_module_in_bin() {
       local binary="$1"
       local module_name="$2"

           && "$binary" list-modules 2>/dev/null | grep -q "^${module_name}\$"; then

       fi
 ```

 ```bash
   fm_version_tag() - keep

   # Probe a runtime extension inside a frankenphp binary's embedded PHP
   # (script-file mode: the embedded php-cli accepts no -r/-d flags).
   # String contract: yes/no.
   fm_embedded_extension_loaded() {
 ```

 Hmm, if command - that's a command exit status test, not a function return value. "二
 进制直接对文件检测" - probing the binary by running it IS the direct detection. I

 ```bash
   fm_embedded_extension_loaded() {
       local binary="$1"
       local extension="$2"
       local probe=""
       local result=""

       if [ -z "$binary" ] || [ ! -x "$binary" ]; then
           echo "no"
           return 0
       fi
       probe="$(mktemp)"
       printf '<?php echo extension_loaded(getenv("FM_PROBE_EXTENSION")) ? "yes" :
 "no";' > "$probe"
       result="$(FM_PROBE_EXTENSION="$extension" "$binary" php-cli "$probe"
 2>/dev/null | tail -n1)"
       rm -f "$probe"
       case "$result" in
           yes) echo "yes" ;;
           *) echo "no" ;;
       esac
   }
 ```

 Good - no exit codes at all.

 Now fm_ensure_dnspod_module. The embedded-runtime completeness floor needs ALL
 required extensions - let me make a helper:

 ```bash
   # Embedded-runtime completeness floor for the compile variant: the dnspod
   # module (DNS-01) plus the extensions the operating scripts depend on
   # (phar+simplexml: composer, pcntl: worker control). String contract:
   # yes/no.
   fm_binary_compile_complete() {
       local binary="$1"
       if [ "$(fm_module_in_bin "$binary" "$FRANKENPHP_DNSPOD_MODULE")" = "yes" ] \
           && [ "$(fm_embedded_extension_loaded "$binary" phar)" = "yes" ] \
           && [ "$(fm_embedded_extension_loaded "$binary" simplexml)" = "yes" ] \
           && [ "$(fm_embedded_extension_loaded "$binary" pcntl)" = "yes" ]; then
           echo "yes"
       else
           echo "no"
       fi
   }
 ```

 Candidate install (extracted, fine-grained):

 ```bash
   # Install a staged static-build candidate into the compiled runtime path
   # (variant takeover): one-time backup, atomic replace, canonical link
   # flip, php shim re-target, variant mutex cleanup, state store. Every
   # sub-step probes first; all outcomes reported by file state.
   fm_dnspod_candidate_install() {
       local candidate="$1"
       local binary=""

       if [ -z "$candidate" ] || [ ! -x "$candidate" ]; then
           echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] [WARN] no staged candidate to install"
           return 0
       fi
       binary="$FRANKENPHP_COMPILED_BINARY_PATH"
       # One-time backup of the pre-dnspod binary; atomic replace via mv (a
       # running octane keeps its old mapping - no ETXTBSY).
       if [ -f "$binary" ] && [ ! -f "${binary}${FRANKENPHP_BACKUP_SUFFIX}" ]; then
           $USE_SUDO cp "$binary" "${binary}${FRANKENPHP_BACKUP_SUFFIX}"
       fi
       $USE_SUDO mkdir -p "$(dirname "$binary")"
       $USE_SUDO cp "$candidate" "${binary}.dnspod-new"
       $USE_SUDO chmod 755 "${binary}.dnspod-new"
       $USE_SUDO mv -f "${binary}.dnspod-new" "$binary"
       rm -rf "$(dirname "$candidate")"
       echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] dnspod module embedded (backup:
 ${binary}${FRANKENPHP_BACKUP_SUFFIX}) ($("$binary" version 2>/dev/null | sed -n
 '1p'))"
       # Variant mutex: the compiled variant now owns the link - remove stale
       # prebuilt variant backup artifacts to keep one active runtime contract.
       rm -f "${FRANKENPHP_PREBUILT_BINARY_PATH}${FRANKENPHP_BACKUP_SUFFIX}"
       fm_variant_set compiled
       fm_ensure_local_bin_link "$binary"
       # The binary identity changed - the php/php-cli shims must re-target it
       # (a stale shim keeps exec'ing the previous binary path, which the apt
       # purge may remove altogether).
       fm_ensure_php_cli_shim
       fm_store_info
   }
 ```

 Wait - the original had candidate sanity BEFORE install:

 ```
   if ! "$candidate" version || ! fm_module_in_bin || ! fm_embedded_extension_loaded
 phar; then
       ERROR; rm -rf dirname; return 1
   fi
 ```

 Keep that as a separate fine-grained probe step in fm_ensure_dnspod_module (or inside
 candidate_install). Let me put the sanity probe inside candidate_install at the top:

 ```bash
       # Candidate sanity BEFORE replacing the live binary: must execute and
       # carry the module + the composer-critical phar extension.
       if [ "$(...)" ... ]; then
           echo ERROR; rm -rf "$(dirname "$candidate")"; return 0
       fi
 ```

 Now fm_ensure_dnspod_module:

 ```bash
   # Ensure the dnspod ACME DNS-01 module is embedded in the ACTIVE frankenphp
   # binary (compile-variant convergence). Fine-grained probes, each an
   # independent step - no step's no-op or deferral gates the next:
   #   1) active binary usable? (fm_install owns the baseline)
   #   2) module + extensions already embedded -> converged no-op
   #   3) variant gate: only the COMPILED variant rebuilds (the prebuilt
   #      variant's DNS-01 contract is acme.sh; an unrecorded host never
   #      surprises a service start with a static build)
   #   4) official static rebuild (fm_static_build) -> candidate install
   # A failed build defers with a warning - the built-in ACME
   # (TLS-ALPN-01/HTTP-01) keeps issuing certificates meanwhile. Once the
   # module has converged, the apt PHP stack is purged: the static binary is
   # the frankenphp plane's ONLY PHP runtime.
   fm_ensure_dnspod_module() {
       local binary=""
       local candidate=""

       # Mirror the shared secret-file token into the runtime store first
       # (idempotent; covers the 93 compile path, 175 re-uses fm_dns01_ensure).
       fm_dnspod_token_ensure
       binary="$(fm_get_binary)"
       if [ "$(fm_binary_usable "$binary")" != "yes" ]; then
           echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] [WARN] no usable frankenphp binary; dnspod
 convergence deferred (run fm_install first)"
           return 0
       fi
       if [ "$(fm_binary_compile_complete "$binary")" = "yes" ]; then
           echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] dnspod module already embedded (phar/pcntl present)"
           fm_ensure_local_bin_link "$binary"
           fm_disable_legacy_php_runtime
           fm_static_apt_php_cleanup
           return 0
       fi

           echo "[$SCRIPT_INDEX] dnspod module not embedded; variant '$(fm_variant)'
 does not rebuild (prebuilt: acme.sh DNS-01; use 93 --compile for the static build)"
           return 0
       fi

 failed; the stderr log above carries the kept workdir path)"
           return 0
       fi
       fm_dnspod_candidate_install "$candidate"
       fm_disable_legacy_php_runtime
       fm_static_apt_php_cleanup
   }
 ```

 Now the static builder rewrite:

 ```bash
   # Host Go toolchain probe/ensure: build-static.sh's xcaddy step needs a
   # `go` on PATH (spc ships its own xcaddy binary, NOT its own Go). Probe
   # PATH first, then the pinned gvar toolchain (91_install_golang.sh),
   # linking it into /usr/local/bin when missing (file-probe idempotent).
   # String contract: the go binary path (empty when unavailable).
   fm_static_go_ensure() {
       local go_bin=""

       if command -v go >/dev/null 2>&1; then
           command -v go
           return 0
       fi
       go_bin="$GO_BIN"
       if [ -x "$go_bin" ]; then
           $USE_SUDO ln -sf "$go_bin" /usr/local/bin/go
           echo "[$FM_STATIC_LOG_TAG] linked /usr/local/bin/go -> $go_bin" >&2
           echo "$go_bin"
           return 0
       fi
       echo "[$FM_STATIC_LOG_TAG] [ERROR] no go toolchain on PATH and no pinned
 toolchain at ${go_bin}" >&2
       echo ""
   }
 ```

 Hmm wait - if go is linked to /usr/local/bin/go but build-static.sh's subshell
 doesn't have /usr/local/bin on PATH? /usr/local/bin is standard on PATH for root and
 users. And in fm_static_build I export PATH to include dirname of resolved go. Let me
 do that: in fm_static_build, resolve go via fm_static_go_ensure; if non-empty, export
 PATH="(dirname):PATH" for the build. Good - deterministic regardless of shell

 Now the reuse probe:

 ```bash
   # Staged-candidate reuse probe: a dist binary from a previous build is
   # reusable when it matches the pinned frankenphp tag and carries the
   # dnspod module plus every configured extension (file probes only - no
   # fingerprint cache can go stale). String contract: yes/no.
   fm_static_dist_reusable() {
       local built_bin="$1"
       local version_tag="$2"
       local extensions="$3"
       local dist_tag=""
       local ext=""

       if [ -z "$built_bin" ] || [ ! -x "$built_bin" ]; then
           echo "no"
           return 0
       fi
       if [ "$(fm_binary_usable "$built_bin")" != "yes" ]; then
           echo "no"
           return 0
       fi
       dist_tag="$(sed -n 's/^FrankenPHP
 \(v[0-9][0-9]*\.[0-9][0-9]*\.[0-9][0-9]*\).*/\1/p' <<<"$("$built_bin" version
 2>/dev/null)" | head -n1)"
       if [ "$dist_tag" != "$version_tag" ]; then
           echo "no"
           return 0
       fi
       if [ "$(fm_module_in_bin "$built_bin" "$FRANKENPHP_DNSPOD_MODULE")" != "yes" ];
 then
           echo "no"
           return 0
       fi
       for ext in $(echo "$extensions" | tr ',' ' '); do
           if [ "$(fm_embedded_extension_loaded "$built_bin" "$ext")" != "yes" ]; then
               echo "no"
               return 0
           fi
       done
       echo "yes"
   }
 ```

 Hmm - wait, <<<"$(...)" heredoc with sed - fine in bash. Actually simpler: reuse
 fm_version_tag? fm_version_tag reads the ACTIVE binary, not an arbitrary one. Let me
 make a helper fm_version_tag_of <binary>:

 Actually fm_version_tag is:

 ```bash
   fm_version_tag() {
       fm_version | sed -n 's/^FrankenPHP \(v...\).*/\1/p' | head -n 1
   }
 ```

 Add:

 ```bash
   # Version tag of an arbitrary binary (empty when unparsable).
   fm_version_tag_of() {
       [ -n "$1" ] && [ -x "$1" ] && "$1" version 2>/dev/null | sed -n 's/^FrankenPHP
 \(v[0-9][0-9]*\.[0-9][0-9]*\.[0-9][0-9]*\).*/\1/p' | head -n 1
   }
   fm_version_tag() {
       fm_version_tag_of "$(fm_get_binary)"
   }
 ```

 Good - dedup (merge duplicate implementations per user rules).

 ```bash
   fm_static_build() {
       local version_tag=""
       local php_ver=""
       local extensions=""
       local embed_dir=""
       local built_bin=""
       local go_bin=""

       if [ "$(fm_static_prereq_ensure)" != "ok" ]; then
           echo "[$FM_STATIC_LOG_TAG] [WARN] static-build prerequisites unsatisfied;
 build deferred" >&2
           echo ""
           return 0
       fi
       go_bin="$(fm_static_go_ensure)"
       if [ -z "$go_bin" ]; then
           echo "[$FM_STATIC_LOG_TAG] [WARN] no usable Go toolchain; build deferred"
 >&2
           echo ""
           return 0
       fi

       version_tag="$(fm_version_tag)"
       ...same guards...
       extensions="$(fm_static_php_extensions)"
       embed_dir="$(fm_static_embed_dir)"

       mkdir -p "$FRANKENPHP_STATIC_BUILD_ROOT"
       built_bin="${FRANKENPHP_STATIC_SRC_DIR}/dist/frankenphp-linux-$(uname -m)"

       # Reuse probe: a matching dist binary from a previous build skips the
       # expensive rebuild entirely (fine-grained idempotency).
       if [ "$(fm_static_dist_reusable "$built_bin" "$version_tag" "$extensions")" =
 "yes" ]; then
           echo "[$FM_STATIC_LOG_TAG] reusing dist binary ${built_bin} (frankenphp
 ${version_tag}, dnspod + extensions verified)" >&2

           ... run build-static.sh (with PATH export) ...
           if [ ! -x "$built_bin" ]; then WARN; echo ""; return 0; fi

       # staging...
       rm -rf "$FRANKENPHP_STATIC_STAGING_DIR"
       mkdir -p "$FRANKENPHP_STATIC_STAGING_DIR"
       cp "$built_bin" "${FRANKENPHP_STATIC_STAGING_DIR}/frankenphp"
       chmod 755 "${FRANKENPHP_STATIC_STAGING_DIR}/frankenphp"
       echo "${FRANKENPHP_STATIC_STAGING_DIR}/frankenphp"
   }
 ```

 Let me write fm_static_prereq_ensure:

 ```bash
   fm_static_prereq_ensure() {
       local os_id=""
       local codename=""
       local missing=""
       local no_cand=""

       os_id="$(apt_sources_restore_os_id)"
       case "$os_id" in
           debian|ubuntu|kali) ;;
           *)
               echo "[$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_TAG] [ERROR] unsupported OS id
 '${os_id:-unknown}' (need debian/ubuntu/kali)" >&2
               echo "failed"
               return 0
               ;;
       esac
       codename="$(apt_sources_restore_codename)"
       if [ -z "$codename" ]; then
           echo "[$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_TAG] [ERROR] cannot resolve the ${os_id} release
 codename" >&2
           echo "failed"
           return 0
       fi

       missing="$(fm_static_prereq_missing "$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_PKGS")"
       if [ -z "$missing" ]; then
           echo "[$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_TAG] prerequisites already satisfied (no apt
 action)" >&2
           echo "ok"
           return 0
       fi
       echo "[$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_TAG] missing packages: $(echo "$missing" | tr '\n' '
 ')" >&2

       apt_sources_restore_ensure 1>&2
       no_cand="$(fm_static_prereq_no_candidate "$missing")"
       if [ -n "$no_cand" ] || [ "$APT_SOURCES_RESTORE_CHANGED" = "true" ]; then
           if [ -n "$no_cand" ]; then
               echo "[$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_TAG] packages without repository candidate:
 $(echo "$no_cand" | tr '\n' ' ')" >&2
           fi
           echo "[$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_TAG] refreshing apt package lists" >&2
           if ! $USE_SUDO apt-get update 1>&2; then
               echo "[$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_TAG] [WARN] apt-get update reported errors
 (continuing)" >&2
           fi
           no_cand="$(fm_static_prereq_no_candidate "$missing")"
           if [ -n "$no_cand" ]; then
               echo "[$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_TAG] [ERROR] still no installation candidate
 after sources heal: $(echo "$no_cand" | tr '\n' ' ')" >&2
               echo "failed"
               return 0
           fi
       fi

       # shellcheck disable=SC2086
       if ! $USE_SUDO apt-get install -y $missing 1>&2; then
           echo "[$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_TAG] [ERROR] apt-get install failed" >&2
           echo "failed"
           return 0
       fi

       missing="$(fm_static_prereq_missing "$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_PKGS")"
       if [ -n "$missing" ]; then
           echo "[$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_TAG] [ERROR] packages still missing after install:
 $(echo "$missing" | tr '\n' ' ')" >&2
           echo "failed"
           return 0
       fi
       echo "[$FM_STATIC_PREREQ_TAG] prerequisites satisfied (spc doctor --auto-fix
 will find a ready host)" >&2
       echo "ok"
   }
 ```

 #!/bin/bash
 # ### AI SPECIAL ATTENTION RULES START ###
 # When AI/ALL DEVELOPERS sees this prompt, MUST IMMEDIATELY COMPLY WITH THESE RULES:
 # 1. Write all code in English only.

 # VIOLATION OF THESE RULES IS STRICTLY PROHIBITED
